# Defensive PHP Workshop — v1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build v1 of the movie marathon planner (Symfony 8.1, FrankenPHP, MySQL). It must be clean, working and secure, and its contracts deliberately stay implicit. It is the starting point of the October 8, 2026 workshop.

**Architecture:**
- A "typical" layered Symfony project: Doctrine entities with getters and setters, application services that exchange arrays, repositories, and thin Twig and JSON controllers that call the same services.
- Pathé is reached only through a framework-free SDK (`src/Sdk/Pathe`, on PSR interfaces), and only by the sync. The site reads the database.
- Instants are stored in UTC and converted with the time zone of each cinema chain.
- A SQL dump of the catalog, prepared the day before, makes the application usable without a network.

**Tech Stack:**
- Runtime: PHP 8.5 on FrankenPHP 1.13.0 Alpine (worker mode, built-in Mercure 1.0 hub), Symfony 8.1, PIE 1.5.
- Data: Doctrine ORM 3.7 / DBAL 4, MySQL 8.4.
- Sign-in: KnpU OAuth2 Client with the league providers, navikt/mock-oauth2-server for the local provider.
- Front and API: Tailwind CSS v4 (standalone CLI through symfonycasts/tailwind-bundle, no Node.js), Symfony UX (Twig Components, Turbo, Autocomplete, Stimulus) with AssetMapper, Symfony Translation, Nelmio API Doc.
- Pathé SDK: PSR-18/17/6/3 with psr-discovery, nyholm/psr7.
- Quality: PHPUnit 13 with dama/doctrine-test-bundle, PHPStan 2, PHP-CS-Fixer.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-04-workshop-design.md`. Read it before any task, sections 5 to 9 in particular.

## Global Constraints

**Platform**
- Base image: `dunglas/frankenphp:1.13.0-php8.5-alpine`, pinned by digest in the Dockerfile (`sha256:b64048cc72ee412fd7247f45d70011c385850c0bfd0b467cd26cdccc99e158ca` on October 4, 2026).
- Mercure 1.0 (built into FrankenPHP 1.13): the hub trusts one token issuer (`issuer` block in the Caddyfile) and Symfony speaks `protocol_version: '1.0'` (mercure-bundle 0.5).
- A single multistage `Dockerfile`:
  - common base: intl, opcache and pdo_mysql through `install-php-extensions` (provided by the image, for the extensions shipped with PHP); apcu 5.1.28 through PIE 1.5.1;
  - `dev` target: Composer, zip, Xdebug 3.5.3 through PIE;
  - `prod` target: no Composer or git, `www-data` user, production OPcache.
- Docker files live under `docker/` (`docker/frankenphp/`, `docker/mysql/`, `docker/oidc/`).
- Versions: Symfony 8.1.*, PHP 8.5, MySQL 8.4 (`mysql:8.4`), `symfony/polyfill-php86`, `symfony/polyfill-time`.
- All commands go through Docker via the `Makefile`. Never run PHP on the host.

**Data and time**
- Generated identifiers: UUID v7 (`Symfony\Component\Uid\Uuid::v7()`).
- Pathé data: the slug is the primary key. A showtime is identified by the `V{n}S{n}` segment of its `refCmd`.
- Time:
  - `date.timezone = UTC`, and the database stores UTC instants.
  - Each showtime also keeps its `localDate`, the calendar day of the cinema.
  - Each cinema has the `timezone` and `country` of its chain, declared in `app.chains` (`config/packages/chains.yaml`).
  - Local times are converted to UTC at the SDK boundary (`PatheMapper::mapShowtimes(..., $timezone)`) and back to the cinema's time zone for display (`H:i`) and in the API (ISO 8601 with offset).
  - No code may hard-code `Europe/Paris`: it appears only in `chains.yaml` and in test data.
- Chains: v1 supports `pathe` only, but every chain-specific value (name, country, time zone) is read from `app.chains`. A future chain gets its own SDK under `src/Sdk/<Chain>/`.

**Pathé SDK**
- `src/Sdk/Pathe` is written like an external library. It imports only `Psr\*`, `PsrDiscovery\*` and its own namespace: no Symfony, no Doctrine, no other `App\` code.
- Its collaborators (PSR-18 client, PSR-17 request factory, PSR-6 cache, PSR-3 logger) are optional constructor arguments. A missing one is discovered with psr-discovery; the application wires them explicitly.
- Pathé access: base `https://www.pathe.fr/api/`, User-Agent `PatheApiExplorer/1.0`.
- Requests are sequential (default delay 1000 ms). Reference data (cities, cinemas, films) is cached `PATHE_CACHE_TTL` seconds. The sync stops on 403/429.

**Web interface and design system**
- Tailwind CSS v4: the standalone CLI v4.3.3 is pinned by checksum in the image and used through `symfonycasts/tailwind-bundle` (`binary` option). Nothing is downloaded at runtime.
- The design system (Task 6) holds the tokens in `@theme` (`assets/styles/app.css`, default palette removed), the anonymous Twig components `Button`, `Card`, `Alert` and `Badge`, and the form theme. It is documented in `docs/design-system.md`, with a style guide at `/design-system`.
- Templates use the components and the token utilities only. Semantic classes (`flash-error`, `programme`, `plan-message`, ids such as `#connections`) are hooks for tests and scripts, never styled.
- System fonts only (offline).

**Language and commits**
- Identifiers, messages, comments, documentation, README and commits are all in English.
- The UI is translated into French with Symfony Translation; English stays the main language (`default_locale: en`, `enabled_locales: [en, fr]`, chosen from `Accept-Language`).
  - The English strings are the translation keys, in `translations/messages.fr.yaml` and `translations/validators.fr.yaml`.
  - Flash messages are translated where they are added (`TranslatorInterface`).
  - Real Pathé data (titles, genres, addresses) is never translated.
- Commits: Conventional Commits in English, signed, author `Thomas Dutrion <hello@tdutrion.fr>` (already configured in the repository). No `Co-Authored-By` trailer.

**Tests**
- Every collaborator is built with a test builder (`tests/Builder/`, immutable, `aX()->withY()->build()`): entities, the fake Pathé API, the fake OAuth provider, the planner inputs.
- Builders produce unsaved entities; `App\Tests\StoresEntities::store()` persists them.
- Prefer real objects over mocks.
- Every test is laid out as `// Arrange`, `// Act`, `// Assert`.
- Only essential behavior tests: use cases, HTTP journeys, database outcomes, security rules. Never test implementation details (mappers, repositories, URL building) on their own.

**v1 style (spec §8)**
- The v1 style is **intentional**: do not "fix" it.
  - No `declare(strict_types=1)`.
  - Associative arrays between services, and scalars for business concepts.
  - `X|false` / `?X` returns and generic exceptions (`\RuntimeException`, `\InvalidArgumentException`).
  - Entities with nullable getters and setters, and `match` on strings.
  - `new \DateTimeImmutable('now', …)` inside services.
- The code must nonetheless be clean, readable, formatted (`@Symfony`) and honest.

**Absolute prohibitions**
- Any security flaw or known bug.
- Logic duplicated between the web and the API.
- User-specific state in a service (worker mode).
- A red test or a PHPStan level 5 error.

**Security in place from v1 (spec §7)**
- Parameterized queries, and Twig escaping.
- CSRF on every form that changes state and on logout.
- OAuth state + PKCE, and account linking only on a verified email.
- Random API tokens stored hashed, and `#[\SensitiveParameter]` on secrets and tokens.
- No redirect to a user-supplied URL.

**Planner rules (spec §5)**
- 10 min margin between showtimes.
- Travel time: straight-line distance at 15 km/h.
- The ads option allows arriving up to `start + 15 min`.
- Never the same film twice.
- Radius 1–50 km (10 by default), 2–5 films.
- At most 3 programmes, pairwise different, with a 20,000 node cap.

## Review Focus

1. **A showtime that ends after midnight.** A 21:40 film ends at 00:09 the next day. It must stay a showtime of its local day and correctly block the next chaining.
   - Tests: Task 3 (`testCopiesTheRequestedCitiesIntoTheCatalogWithInstantsInUtc`: `localDate` stays October 4) and Task 8 (`testChainsAcrossMidnight`).
2. **Local time vs UTC.** A user reads the cinema's local time everywhere, whatever the server time zone, and each cinema follows its own chain's zone.
   - Tests: Task 9 (`testPlansTheThreeBestDistinctProgrammesInLocalTime`, `testShowsTheTimesOfEachCinemaInItsOwnTimeZone`) and Task 12 (`testPlansThreeProgrammesWithTheTimesOfTheCinema`, ISO offset `+01:00`).
3. **A city whose Pathé position is wrong** (Dijon). Its centre must be the barycentre of its cinemas, otherwise there is no result.
   - Test: Task 9 (`testACityCentreIsTheBarycentreOfItsCinemas`).
4. **A user who has already seen almost everything.** They get fewer than 3 programmes, or even none, with an explicit message instead of an empty page or an error.
   - Tests: Task 9 (`testExplainsWhyThereAreFewerThanThreeProgrammes`) and Task 10 (`testExplainsWhyNothingCanBePlanned`).
5. **A reloaded or forged OAuth callback.** The user is sent back to the sign-in page with a message in their language, never shown a 500 error or signed into someone else's account.
   - Tests: Task 7 (`testAForgedStateIsRefusedInTheLanguageOfTheBrowser`, `testACallbackThisBrowserNeverStartedSendsBackToTheSignInPage`, `testAnUnverifiedGithubEmailDoesNotOpenTheAccountUsingIt`).

---

## Tasks

### Task 1: Foundation — Docker (FrankenPHP Alpine, MySQL), Symfony 8.1 project, tooling

**Files:**
- Create: `Dockerfile`, `.dockerignore`, `compose.yaml`, `compose.override.yaml`, `Makefile`
- Create: `docker/frankenphp/Caddyfile`, `docker/frankenphp/docker-entrypoint.sh`, `docker/frankenphp/conf.d/10-app.ini`, `docker/frankenphp/conf.d/20-app.dev.ini`, `docker/frankenphp/conf.d/20-app.prod.ini`
- Create: `docker/mysql/init/01-test-database.sql`
- Create (via Composer/Flex): the Symfony 8.1 skeleton (`composer.json`, `bin/`, `config/`, `public/`, `src/Kernel.php`, `templates/`, `assets/`, `tests/`, `phpunit.dist.xml`, `.php-cs-fixer.dist.php`…)
- Create: `phpstan.dist.neon`, `src/Catalog/Entity/.gitkeep`, `src/Account/Entity/.gitkeep`
- Modify: `config/packages/doctrine.yaml`, `config/packages/framework.yaml` (locales), `config/services.yaml`, `config/packages/twig.yaml`, `phpunit.dist.xml`, `.env`, `.gitignore`
- Test: `tests/Functional/SmokeTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing (repository containing only `docs/` and `.gitignore`).
- Produces:
  - `make up | down | logs | sh | composer c=… | console c=… | test [c=…] | phpstan | cs | sync [c=…]`; `PHP` = `docker compose exec -T php` in the Makefile.
  - Dev database `app`, test database `app_test` (Doctrine `_test` suffix), MySQL 8.4 reachable from PHP at `database:3306`.
  - Doctrine mappings: `App\Catalog\Entity` (folder `src/Catalog/Entity`) and `App\Account\Entity` (`src/Account/Entity`).
  - `date.timezone = UTC` everywhere: the application stores and computes in UTC, and converts to a cinema's own time zone only at the edges (Pathé input, display).
  - Locales: default `en`, enabled `en` and `fr`, chosen from the browser's `Accept-Language`. UI strings are English in the code and translated in `translations/messages.fr.yaml` and `translations/validators.fr.yaml` (created by the first task that needs them).
  - Tests: `KernelTestCase`/`WebTestCase`, each test running in a rolled-back transaction (dama/doctrine-test-bundle).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the Dockerfile**

`Dockerfile` (adapted from dunglas/symfony-docker and its `alpine.md` doc; `base` and `dev` stages verified in real conditions on October 4, 2026):

```dockerfile
#syntax=docker/dockerfile:1

# Versions (the digest is added in step 2 of this plan)
FROM dunglas/frankenphp:1.13.0-php8.5-alpine AS frankenphp_upstream


# Base image
FROM frankenphp_upstream AS frankenphp_base

SHELL ["/bin/ash", "-eux", "-o", "pipefail", "-c"]

WORKDIR /app

# Permanent dependencies, Composer, and extensions shipped with PHP
# hadolint ignore=DL3018
RUN <<-EOF
	apk add --no-cache file git
	install-php-extensions @composer intl opcache pdo_mysql
EOF

# Pinned APCu, installed with PIE (build dependencies removed afterwards)
# hadolint ignore=DL3018
RUN --mount=type=bind,from=ghcr.io/php/pie:1.5.1-bin,source=/pie,target=/usr/local/bin/pie <<-EOF
	apk add --no-cache --virtual .pie-build-deps $PHPIZE_DEPS linux-headers
	pie install --no-cache apcu/apcu:5.1.28
	apk del --no-network .pie-build-deps
	rm -rf /config/pie
EOF

# https://getcomposer.org/doc/03-cli.md#composer-allow-superuser
ENV COMPOSER_ALLOW_SUPERUSER=1

ENV PHP_INI_SCAN_DIR=":$PHP_INI_DIR/app.conf.d"

COPY --link docker/frankenphp/conf.d/10-app.ini $PHP_INI_DIR/app.conf.d/
COPY --link --chmod=755 docker/frankenphp/docker-entrypoint.sh /usr/local/bin/docker-entrypoint
COPY --link docker/frankenphp/Caddyfile /etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile

ENTRYPOINT ["docker-entrypoint"]

HEALTHCHECK --start-period=60s CMD php -r 'exit(false === @file_get_contents("http://localhost:2019/metrics", context: stream_context_create(["http" => ["timeout" => 5]])) ? 1 : 0);'
CMD [ "frankenphp", "run", "--config", "/etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile" ]


# Development image: Composer, zip, Xdebug
FROM frankenphp_base AS frankenphp_dev

ENV APP_ENV=dev
ENV XDEBUG_MODE=off
ENV FRANKENPHP_WORKER_CONFIG=watch

# hadolint ignore=DL3018
RUN --mount=type=bind,from=ghcr.io/php/pie:1.5.1-bin,source=/pie,target=/usr/local/bin/pie <<-EOF
	mv "$PHP_INI_DIR/php.ini-development" "$PHP_INI_DIR/php.ini"
	install-php-extensions zip
	apk add --no-cache --virtual .pie-build-deps $PHPIZE_DEPS linux-headers
	pie install --no-cache xdebug/xdebug:3.5.3
	apk del --no-network .pie-build-deps
	rm -rf /config/pie
	adduser -D -s /bin/ash nonroot
	git config --system --add safe.directory /app
EOF

COPY --link docker/frankenphp/conf.d/20-app.dev.ini $PHP_INI_DIR/app.conf.d/

CMD [ "frankenphp", "run", "--config", "/etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile", "--watch" ]


# Production image: no development tools, non-root user
FROM frankenphp_base AS frankenphp_prod

ENV APP_ENV=prod

RUN mv "$PHP_INI_DIR/php.ini-production" "$PHP_INI_DIR/php.ini"

COPY --link docker/frankenphp/conf.d/20-app.prod.ini $PHP_INI_DIR/app.conf.d/

# Dependencies are only reinstalled if composer.* changes
COPY --link composer.* symfony.* ./
RUN composer install --no-cache --prefer-dist --no-dev --no-autoloader --no-scripts --no-progress

COPY --link --exclude=docker/ . ./

# hadolint ignore=DL3018
RUN <<-EOF
	mkdir -p var/cache var/log var/share
	composer dump-autoload --classmap-authoritative --no-dev
	composer dump-env prod
	composer run-script --no-dev post-install-cmd
	php bin/console importmap:install
	php bin/console asset-map:compile
	chmod +x bin/console
	rm -f /usr/local/bin/composer
	apk add --no-cache libcap-setcap
	setcap CAP_NET_BIND_SERVICE=+eip /usr/local/bin/frankenphp
	apk del --no-network git libcap-setcap
	chown -R www-data:www-data /data/caddy /config/caddy var
EOF

USER www-data
```

Expected: `"sha256:b64048cc72ee412fd7247f45d70011c385850c0bfd0b467cd26cdccc99e158ca"` (value of October 4, 2026; take the value obtained if the tag was rebuilt). Replace the first `FROM` line with `FROM dunglas/frankenphp:1.13.0-php8.5-alpine@sha256:<value obtained> AS frankenphp_upstream`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the FrankenPHP configuration**

`docker/frankenphp/Caddyfile` (the one from symfony-docker, without the Mercure subscription API or the demo UI):

```caddyfile
{
	skip_install_trust

	{$CADDY_GLOBAL_OPTIONS}

	frankenphp {
		{$FRANKENPHP_CONFIG}
	}
}

{$CADDY_EXTRA_CONFIG}

{$SERVER_NAME:localhost} {
	log {
		{$CADDY_SERVER_LOG_OPTIONS}
		# Hides the authorization parameter that Mercure may use
		format filter {
			request>uri query {
				replace authorization REDACTED
			}
		}
	}

	root /app/public
	encode zstd br gzip

	mercure {
		# Mercure 1.0: tokens must carry this "iss" claim (set by config/packages/mercure.yaml).
		issuer {$MERCURE_TRUSTED_ISSUERS:https://localhost} {
			publisher {
				jwt {env.MERCURE_PUBLISHER_JWT_KEY} {env.MERCURE_PUBLISHER_JWT_ALG}
			}
			subscriber {
				jwt {env.MERCURE_SUBSCRIBER_JWT_KEY} {env.MERCURE_SUBSCRIBER_JWT_ALG}
			}
		}
		# PHP publishes through http://php, browsers subscribe through https://localhost:
		# one fixed audience ("aud" claim) for both.
		resource_identifier {$MERCURE_RESOURCE_IDENTIFIER:https://localhost/.well-known/mercure}
		# Only public updates are emitted ("programme updated")
		anonymous
		{$MERCURE_EXTRA_DIRECTIVES}
	}

	vulcain

	{$CADDY_SERVER_EXTRA_DIRECTIVES}

	@phpRoute {
		not path /.well-known/mercure*
		not file {path}
	}
	rewrite @phpRoute index.php

	@frontController path index.php
	php @frontController {
		{$FRANKENPHP_SITE_CONFIG}

		worker {
			file ./public/index.php
			{$FRANKENPHP_WORKER_CONFIG}
		}
	}

	file_server {
		hide *.php
	}
}
```

`docker/frankenphp/docker-entrypoint.sh` (the one from symfony-docker, without the initial installation block):

```sh
#!/bin/sh
set -e

if [ "$1" = 'frankenphp' ] || [ "$1" = 'php' ] || [ "$1" = 'bin/console' ]; then
	if [ -z "$(ls -A 'vendor/' 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
		composer install --prefer-dist --no-progress --no-interaction
	fi

	php bin/console -V

	if grep -q ^DATABASE_URL= .env; then
		echo 'Waiting for database to be ready...'
		ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE=60
		until [ $ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE -eq 0 ] || DATABASE_ERROR=$(php bin/console dbal:run-sql -q "SELECT 1" 2>&1); do
			if [ $? -eq 255 ]; then
				ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE=0
				break
			fi
			sleep 1
			ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE=$((ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE - 1))
			echo "Still waiting for database to be ready... Or maybe the database is not reachable. $ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE attempts left."
		done

		if [ $ATTEMPTS_LEFT_TO_REACH_DATABASE -eq 0 ]; then
			echo 'The database is not up or not reachable:'
			echo "$DATABASE_ERROR"
			exit 1
		else
			echo 'The database is now ready and reachable'
		fi

		if find ./migrations -iname '*.php' -print -quit | grep --quiet .; then
			php bin/console doctrine:migrations:migrate --no-interaction --all-or-nothing
		fi
	fi

	echo 'PHP app ready!'
fi

exec docker-php-entrypoint "$@"
```

`docker/frankenphp/conf.d/10-app.ini`:

```ini
expose_php = 0
date.timezone = UTC

; https://symfony.com/doc/current/performance.html
realpath_cache_size = 4096K
realpath_cache_ttl = 600
opcache.interned_strings_buffer = 32
opcache.max_accelerated_files = 32531
opcache.memory_consumption = 256
opcache.enable_file_override = 1
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write Compose, the MySQL initialization and the Makefile**

`compose.yaml`:

```yaml
x-php-env: &php-env
  DATABASE_URL: mysql://${MYSQL_USER:-app}:${MYSQL_PASSWORD:-app-dev-password}@database:3306/${MYSQL_DATABASE:-app}?serverVersion=8.4.0&charset=utf8mb4
  MERCURE_URL: http://php/.well-known/mercure
  MERCURE_PUBLIC_URL: https://${SERVER_NAME:-localhost}:${HTTPS_PORT:-443}/.well-known/mercure
  MERCURE_JWT_SECRET: ${CADDY_MERCURE_JWT_SECRET:-workshop-local-only-mercure-secret-0123456789}

services:
  php:
    image: ${IMAGES_PREFIX:-}workshop-php
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      <<: *php-env
      SERVER_NAME: ${SERVER_NAME:-localhost}, php:80
      DEFAULT_URI: https://${SERVER_NAME:-localhost}:${HTTPS_PORT:-443}
      MERCURE_PUBLISHER_JWT_KEY: ${CADDY_MERCURE_JWT_SECRET:-workshop-local-only-mercure-secret-0123456789}
      MERCURE_SUBSCRIBER_JWT_KEY: ${CADDY_MERCURE_JWT_SECRET:-workshop-local-only-mercure-secret-0123456789}
    depends_on:
      database:
        condition: service_healthy
    volumes:
      - caddy_data:/data
      - caddy_config:/config
    ports:
      - name: http
        target: 80
        published: ${HTTP_PORT:-80}
        protocol: tcp
      - name: https
        target: 443
        published: ${HTTPS_PORT:-443}
        protocol: tcp
      - name: http3
        target: 443
        published: ${HTTP3_PORT:-443}
        protocol: udp

  database:
    image: mysql:8.4
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      MYSQL_DATABASE: ${MYSQL_DATABASE:-app}
      MYSQL_USER: ${MYSQL_USER:-app}
      MYSQL_PASSWORD: ${MYSQL_PASSWORD:-app-dev-password}
      MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD:-root-dev-password}
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "mysqladmin", "ping", "-h", "127.0.0.1", "--silent"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20
      start_period: 30s
    volumes:
      - database_data:/var/lib/mysql
      - ./docker/mysql/init:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d:ro

volumes:
  caddy_data:
  caddy_config:
  database_data:
```

`compose.override.yaml`:

```yaml
# Development environment
services:
  php:
    image: ${IMAGES_PREFIX:-}workshop-php-dev
    build:
      context: .
      target: frankenphp_dev
    volumes:
      - ./:/app
      - ./docker/frankenphp/Caddyfile:/etc/frankenphp/Caddyfile:ro
      - ./docker/frankenphp/conf.d/20-app.dev.ini:/usr/local/etc/php/app.conf.d/20-app.dev.ini:ro
      # var/ stays outside the mount for better performance on macOS
      - /app/var
    environment:
      FRANKENPHP_WORKER_CONFIG: watch
      XDEBUG_MODE: "${XDEBUG_MODE:-off}"
      APP_ENV: "${APP_ENV:-dev}"
    extra_hosts:
      - host.docker.internal:host-gateway
    tty: true

  database:
    ports:
      - "3306"
```

`docker/mysql/init/01-test-database.sql`:

```sql
-- Database used by PHPUnit (Doctrine adds the "_test" suffix in the test environment).
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS app_test;
GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON app_test.* TO 'app'@'%';
```

`Makefile` (recipes are indented with tabs):

```make
COMPOSE = docker compose
PHP     = $(COMPOSE) exec -T php
CONSOLE = $(PHP) bin/console
c ?=

.DEFAULT_GOAL := help

help: ## Lists the commands
	@grep -E '^[a-zA-Z_-]+:.*?## .*$$' $(MAKEFILE_LIST) | awk 'BEGIN {FS = ":.*?## "}; {printf "\033[36m%-18s\033[0m %s\n", $$1, $$2}'

up: ## Builds and starts the containers
	$(COMPOSE) up --build --wait

down: ## Stops the containers
	$(COMPOSE) down --remove-orphans

logs: ## Follows the logs
	$(COMPOSE) logs -f

sh: ## Opens a shell in the PHP container
	$(COMPOSE) exec php sh

composer: ## Runs Composer, e.g. make composer c="require foo/bar"
	$(PHP) composer $(c)

console: ## Runs the Symfony console, e.g. make console c="cache:clear"
	$(CONSOLE) $(c)

test: ## Runs PHPUnit, e.g. make test c="--filter Planner"
	$(CONSOLE) doctrine:database:create --env=test --if-not-exists
	$(CONSOLE) doctrine:migrations:migrate --env=test --no-interaction --allow-no-migration
	$(PHP) bin/phpunit $(c)

phpstan: ## Static analysis (level 5)
	$(PHP) vendor/bin/phpstan analyse --memory-limit=1G

cs: ## Fixes the code style (@Symfony)
	$(PHP) vendor/bin/php-cs-fixer fix

sync: ## Synchronizes the catalog from pathe.fr, e.g. make sync c="--city=dijon"
	$(CONSOLE) catalog:sync $(c)

.PHONY: help up down logs sh composer console test phpstan cs sync
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Build the dev image**

Run: `docker compose build php`
Expected: build succeeds; then `docker compose run --rm --no-deps php php -m | grep -E 'apcu|intl|pdo_mysql|xdebug|zip'` shows the 5 extensions.

- [ ] **Step 6: Create the Symfony 8.1 skeleton**

The repository is not empty (`docs/`): we create the project in `tmp/` and then copy it over.

Run:

```bash
docker compose run --rm --no-deps php composer create-project symfony/skeleton:"8.1.*" tmp --prefer-dist --no-progress --no-interaction
docker compose run --rm --no-deps php sh -c 'cp -Rp tmp/. . && rm -Rf tmp'
printf '.remember/\n' >> .gitignore
docker compose run --rm --no-deps php composer config --json extra.symfony.docker false
docker compose run --rm --no-deps php composer require --no-interaction webapp symfony/polyfill-php86 symfony/polyfill-time
docker compose run --rm --no-deps php composer require --dev --no-interaction phpstan/phpstan phpstan/extension-installer phpstan/phpstan-symfony phpstan/phpstan-doctrine friendsofphp/php-cs-fixer dama/doctrine-test-bundle
```

Expected: `composer.json` requires `symfony/*` at `8.1.*`; `extra.symfony.docker` is `false` (the Flex recipes touch neither the Dockerfile nor Compose); `.gitignore` contains `.remember/` again.

- [ ] **Step 7: Configure Symfony**

In `.env`, replace the `DATABASE_URL` line created by the recipe with:

```dotenv
DATABASE_URL="mysql://app:app-dev-password@database:3306/app?serverVersion=8.4.0&charset=utf8mb4"
```

(`serverVersion=8.4.0` and not `8.4`: otherwise Doctrine picks the MySQL 8.0 platform.)

`config/packages/doctrine.yaml`, replace the `mappings:` block with:

```yaml
        mappings:
            Catalog:
                type: attribute
                is_bundle: false
                dir: '%kernel.project_dir%/src/Catalog/Entity'
                prefix: 'App\Catalog\Entity'
                alias: Catalog
            Account:
                type: attribute
                is_bundle: false
                dir: '%kernel.project_dir%/src/Account/Entity'
                prefix: 'App\Account\Entity'
                alias: Account
```

Create `src/Catalog/Entity/.gitkeep` and `src/Account/Entity/.gitkeep`. Delete `src/Controller/`, `src/Entity/` and `src/Repository/` created by the recipes.

`config/services.yaml`, in the `App\:` resource, replace the exclusions with:

```yaml
        exclude:
            - '../src/DependencyInjection/'
            - '../src/*/Entity/'
            - '../src/Kernel.php'
```

`config/packages/framework.yaml`, add under `framework:` (English is the main language; French is served to browsers that ask for it):

```yaml
    default_locale: en
    enabled_locales: ['en', 'fr']
    set_locale_from_accept_language: true
    set_content_language_from_locale: true
```

Check that `config/packages/translation.yaml` (Flex recipe) has `default_path: '%kernel.project_dir%/translations'` and `fallbacks: ['en']`.

`config/packages/twig.yaml`: set `strict_variables: false` (intentional v1 style, spec §8) and remove any `when@test` override.

`phpunit.dist.xml`, add inside `<phpunit>`:

```xml
    <extensions>
        <bootstrap class="DAMA\DoctrineTestBundle\PHPUnit\PHPUnitExtension"/>
    </extensions>
```

`phpstan.dist.neon`:

```neon
parameters:
    level: 5
    paths:
        - src
        - tests
    symfony:
        containerXmlPath: var/cache/dev/App_KernelDevDebugContainer.xml
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the smoke test**

`tests/Functional/SmokeTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Functional;

use Doctrine\DBAL\Connection;
use Symfony\Bundle\FrameworkBundle\Test\WebTestCase;

final class SmokeTest extends WebTestCase
{
    public function testTheApplicationAnswersAndTheDatabaseIsReachable(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        $client = self::createClient();
        $connection = self::getContainer()->get(Connection::class);

        // Act
        $client->request('GET', '/a-page-that-does-not-exist');

        // Assert
        self::assertResponseStatusCodeSame(404);
        self::assertSame(1, (int) $connection->fetchOne('SELECT 1'));
        self::assertSame('UTC', date_default_timezone_get());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Start and verify everything**

Run: `make up && make test && make phpstan && make cs`
Expected: `php` and `database` containers healthy; 1 green test; PHPStan with no error; CS with no remaining change.
If PHPUnit reports an error from the dama extension, the PHPUnit version (13) is not yet supported by the bundle: `make composer c="require --dev phpunit/phpunit:^12.5"` then rerun `make test`.

Run: `curl -sk -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}\n' https://localhost/`
Expected: `200` (Symfony welcome page in dev).

Run: `docker build --target frankenphp_prod -t workshop-prod-check .`
Expected: build succeeds (production image without Composer, without git, `www-data` user).

- [ ] **Step 10: Commit**

```bash
git add -A
git status --short   # check: no vendor/, no var/, no .env.local
git commit -m "chore: bootstrap Symfony 8.1 on FrankenPHP Alpine with MySQL 8.4"
```


### Task 2: Catalog — entities, migration, repositories

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Catalog/Entity/City.php`, `src/Catalog/Entity/Cinema.php`, `src/Catalog/Entity/Film.php`, `src/Catalog/Entity/Showtime.php`
- Create: `src/Catalog/Repository/CityRepository.php`, `CinemaRepository.php`, `FilmRepository.php`, `ShowtimeRepository.php`
- Create: `migrations/Version*.php` (generated)
- Create: `tests/StoresEntities.php`, `tests/Builder/CityBuilder.php`, `tests/Builder/CinemaBuilder.php`, `tests/Builder/FilmBuilder.php`, `tests/Builder/ShowtimeBuilder.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 foundation (Doctrine, test database, `make test`).
- Produces:
  - Entities with fluent getters/setters (`setX(): static`): `City(slug, name, chain, country)`, `Cinema(slug, name, chain, country, timezone, city, address, postalCode, town, latitude, longitude, hallCount, open)`, `Film(slug, title, chain, duration, releaseDate, genres, posterUrl, contentRating)`, `Showtime(id, film, cinema, startsAt, endsAt, localDate, version, status, bookingUrl, reservableUntil, auditorium, capacity)`. `startsAt`, `endsAt` and `reservableUntil` are UTC; `localDate` is the session's calendar day in the cinema's time zone.
  - `CityRepository::findAllForSelect(): array` → `list<array{slug: string, name: string}>`; `CityRepository::findBySlug(string $slug): ?array`.
  - `CinemaRepository::findOpenWithCoordinates(): array` → rows `['slug','name','citySlug','timezone','latitude','longitude']`; `CinemaRepository::findByCity(string $citySlug): array` (same keys).
  - `FilmRepository::findBySlug(string $slug): ?array` → `['slug','title','duration','releaseDate','genres','posterUrl','contentRating']`; `FilmRepository::findBySlugs(array $slugs, string $direction = 'ASC'): array` (sorted by title, `'ASC'` or `'DESC'`).
  - `ShowtimeRepository::findAvailableDates(string $from): array` → local days `'Y-m-d'` ≥ `$from`; `ShowtimeRepository::findCandidates(string $date, array $cinemaSlugs, array $excludedFilmSlugs, ?string $version, string $now): array` (`$date` = local day, `$now` = UTC) → rows `['id','filmSlug','filmTitle','duration','cinemaSlug','cinemaName','timezone','latitude','longitude','startsAt','endsAt','version','bookingUrl']` (UTC instants as `Y-m-d H:i:s` strings); `ShowtimeRepository::deleteForCinemasBetween(array $cinemaSlugs, string $fromDate, string $toDate, array $keepIds): int` (local days, inclusive).
  - Test builders (immutable, `build()` returns an unsaved entity): `CityBuilder::aCity()->withSlug()->named()`, `CinemaBuilder::aCinema()->withSlug()->named()->in(City)->at(lat, lng)->inTimezone('Europe/Paris')->closed()`, `FilmBuilder::aFilm()->withSlug()->titled()->lasting(minutes)`, `ShowtimeBuilder::aShowtime()->withId()->of(Film)->at(Cinema)->startingAt('Y-m-d H:i:s' local to the cinema)->inVersion()->bookableUntil('Y-m-d H:i:s' UTC)`.
  - Trait `App\Tests\StoresEntities::store(object ...$entities)` (persist + flush) for kernel and web tests.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the entities**

`src/Catalog/Entity/City.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Entity;

use App\Catalog\Repository\CityRepository;
use Doctrine\ORM\Mapping as ORM;

#[ORM\Entity(repositoryClass: CityRepository::class)]
class City
{
    #[ORM\Id]
    #[ORM\Column(length: 100)]
    private ?string $slug = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 255)]
    private ?string $name = null;

    /** Cinema chain this record comes from, e.g. 'pathe' (see config/packages/chains.yaml). */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 32)]
    private ?string $chain = null;

    /** ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code, e.g. 'FR'. */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 2)]
    private ?string $country = null;

    public function getSlug(): ?string
    {
        return $this->slug;
    }

    public function setSlug(string $slug): static
    {
        $this->slug = $slug;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getName(): ?string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    public function setName(string $name): static
    {
        $this->name = $name;

        return $this;
    }
    public function getChain(): ?string
    {
        return $this->chain;
    }

    public function setChain(string $chain): static
    {
        $this->chain = $chain;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getCountry(): ?string
    {
        return $this->country;
    }

    public function setCountry(string $country): static
    {
        $this->country = $country;

        return $this;
    }

}
```

`src/Catalog/Entity/Cinema.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Entity;

use App\Catalog\Repository\CinemaRepository;
use Doctrine\ORM\Mapping as ORM;

#[ORM\Entity(repositoryClass: CinemaRepository::class)]
class Cinema
{
    #[ORM\Id]
    #[ORM\Column(length: 100)]
    private ?string $slug = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 255)]
    private ?string $name = null;

    /** Cinema chain this record comes from, e.g. 'pathe' (see config/packages/chains.yaml). */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 32)]
    private ?string $chain = null;

    /** ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 country code, e.g. 'FR'. */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 2)]
    private ?string $country = null;

    /** IANA time zone of the cinema, e.g. 'Europe/Paris': showtimes are stored in UTC and shown in this zone. */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 64)]
    private ?string $timezone = null;

    #[ORM\ManyToOne]
    #[ORM\JoinColumn(name: 'city_slug', referencedColumnName: 'slug', nullable: false)]
    private ?City $city = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 255, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $address = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 10, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $postalCode = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 100, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $town = null;

    #[ORM\Column(nullable: true)]
    private ?float $latitude = null;

    #[ORM\Column(nullable: true)]
    private ?float $longitude = null;

    #[ORM\Column(nullable: true)]
    private ?int $hallCount = null;

    #[ORM\Column]
    private bool $open = true;

    public function getSlug(): ?string
    {
        return $this->slug;
    }

    public function setSlug(string $slug): static
    {
        $this->slug = $slug;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getName(): ?string
    {
        return $this->name;
    }

    public function setName(string $name): static
    {
        $this->name = $name;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getCity(): ?City
    {
        return $this->city;
    }

    public function setCity(?City $city): static
    {
        $this->city = $city;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getAddress(): ?string
    {
        return $this->address;
    }

    public function setAddress(?string $address): static
    {
        $this->address = $address;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getPostalCode(): ?string
    {
        return $this->postalCode;
    }

    public function setPostalCode(?string $postalCode): static
    {
        $this->postalCode = $postalCode;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getTown(): ?string
    {
        return $this->town;
    }

    public function setTown(?string $town): static
    {
        $this->town = $town;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getLatitude(): ?float
    {
        return $this->latitude;
    }

    public function setLatitude(?float $latitude): static
    {
        $this->latitude = $latitude;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getLongitude(): ?float
    {
        return $this->longitude;
    }

    public function setLongitude(?float $longitude): static
    {
        $this->longitude = $longitude;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getHallCount(): ?int
    {
        return $this->hallCount;
    }

    public function setHallCount(?int $hallCount): static
    {
        $this->hallCount = $hallCount;

        return $this;
    }

    public function isOpen(): bool
    {
        return $this->open;
    }

    public function setOpen(bool $open): static
    {
        $this->open = $open;

        return $this;
    }
    public function getChain(): ?string
    {
        return $this->chain;
    }

    public function setChain(string $chain): static
    {
        $this->chain = $chain;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getCountry(): ?string
    {
        return $this->country;
    }

    public function setCountry(string $country): static
    {
        $this->country = $country;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getTimezone(): ?string
    {
        return $this->timezone;
    }

    public function setTimezone(string $timezone): static
    {
        $this->timezone = $timezone;

        return $this;
    }

}
```

`src/Catalog/Entity/Film.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Entity;

use App\Catalog\Repository\FilmRepository;
use Doctrine\DBAL\Types\Types;
use Doctrine\ORM\Mapping as ORM;

#[ORM\Entity(repositoryClass: FilmRepository::class)]
class Film
{
    #[ORM\Id]
    #[ORM\Column(length: 150)]
    private ?string $slug = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 255)]
    private ?string $title = null;

    /** Cinema chain this record comes from, e.g. 'pathe' (see config/packages/chains.yaml). */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 32)]
    private ?string $chain = null;

    /** Running time in minutes. */
    #[ORM\Column(nullable: true)]
    private ?int $duration = null;

    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::DATE_IMMUTABLE, nullable: true)]
    private ?\DateTimeImmutable $releaseDate = null;

    /** @var array list of genres, e.g. ['Action', 'Comédie'] */
    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::JSON)]
    private array $genres = [];

    #[ORM\Column(length: 500, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $posterUrl = null;

    /** Rating label, e.g. "Tout public". */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 100, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $contentRating = null;

    public function getSlug(): ?string
    {
        return $this->slug;
    }

    public function setSlug(string $slug): static
    {
        $this->slug = $slug;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getTitle(): ?string
    {
        return $this->title;
    }

    public function setTitle(string $title): static
    {
        $this->title = $title;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getDuration(): ?int
    {
        return $this->duration;
    }

    public function setDuration(?int $duration): static
    {
        $this->duration = $duration;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getReleaseDate(): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->releaseDate;
    }

    public function setReleaseDate(?\DateTimeImmutable $releaseDate): static
    {
        $this->releaseDate = $releaseDate;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getGenres(): array
    {
        return $this->genres;
    }

    public function setGenres(array $genres): static
    {
        $this->genres = $genres;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getPosterUrl(): ?string
    {
        return $this->posterUrl;
    }

    public function setPosterUrl(?string $posterUrl): static
    {
        $this->posterUrl = $posterUrl;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getContentRating(): ?string
    {
        return $this->contentRating;
    }

    public function setContentRating(?string $contentRating): static
    {
        $this->contentRating = $contentRating;

        return $this;
    }
    public function getChain(): ?string
    {
        return $this->chain;
    }

    public function setChain(string $chain): static
    {
        $this->chain = $chain;

        return $this;
    }

}
```

`src/Catalog/Entity/Showtime.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Entity;

use App\Catalog\Repository\ShowtimeRepository;
use Doctrine\DBAL\Types\Types;
use Doctrine\ORM\Mapping as ORM;

#[ORM\Entity(repositoryClass: ShowtimeRepository::class)]
#[ORM\Index(columns: ['starts_at'])]
#[ORM\Index(columns: ['local_date'])]
class Showtime
{
    /** Pathé showtime identifier, e.g. "V3345S85474" (extracted from the booking link). */
    #[ORM\Id]
    #[ORM\Column(length: 32)]
    private ?string $id = null;

    #[ORM\ManyToOne]
    #[ORM\JoinColumn(name: 'film_slug', referencedColumnName: 'slug', nullable: false, onDelete: 'CASCADE')]
    private ?Film $film = null;

    #[ORM\ManyToOne]
    #[ORM\JoinColumn(name: 'cinema_slug', referencedColumnName: 'slug', nullable: false, onDelete: 'CASCADE')]
    private ?Cinema $cinema = null;

    /** Session start (start of the ads), in UTC. */
    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::DATETIME_IMMUTABLE)]
    private ?\DateTimeImmutable $startsAt = null;

    /** End of the film, in UTC. */
    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::DATETIME_IMMUTABLE)]
    private ?\DateTimeImmutable $endsAt = null;

    /** Calendar day of the session in the cinema's time zone: a session starting at 00:30 local time belongs to that day. */
    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::DATE_IMMUTABLE)]
    private ?\DateTimeImmutable $localDate = null;

    /** Version: 'vf', 'vost', 'vo' or 'vfst'. */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 8)]
    private ?string $version = null;

    /** Pathé status, e.g. 'available'. */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 20)]
    private ?string $status = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 255)]
    private ?string $bookingUrl = null;

    #[ORM\Column(type: Types::DATETIME_IMMUTABLE, nullable: true)]
    private ?\DateTimeImmutable $reservableUntil = null;

    #[ORM\Column(length: 50, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $auditorium = null;

    /** Number of seats, as Pathé sends it (string, e.g. "244"). */
    #[ORM\Column(length: 10, nullable: true)]
    private ?string $capacity = null;

    public function getId(): ?string
    {
        return $this->id;
    }

    public function setId(string $id): static
    {
        $this->id = $id;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getFilm(): ?Film
    {
        return $this->film;
    }

    public function setFilm(?Film $film): static
    {
        $this->film = $film;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getCinema(): ?Cinema
    {
        return $this->cinema;
    }

    public function setCinema(?Cinema $cinema): static
    {
        $this->cinema = $cinema;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getStartsAt(): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->startsAt;
    }

    public function setStartsAt(\DateTimeImmutable $startsAt): static
    {
        $this->startsAt = $startsAt;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getEndsAt(): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->endsAt;
    }

    public function setEndsAt(\DateTimeImmutable $endsAt): static
    {
        $this->endsAt = $endsAt;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getVersion(): ?string
    {
        return $this->version;
    }

    public function setVersion(string $version): static
    {
        $this->version = $version;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getStatus(): ?string
    {
        return $this->status;
    }

    public function setStatus(string $status): static
    {
        $this->status = $status;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getBookingUrl(): ?string
    {
        return $this->bookingUrl;
    }

    public function setBookingUrl(string $bookingUrl): static
    {
        $this->bookingUrl = $bookingUrl;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getReservableUntil(): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->reservableUntil;
    }

    public function setReservableUntil(?\DateTimeImmutable $reservableUntil): static
    {
        $this->reservableUntil = $reservableUntil;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getAuditorium(): ?string
    {
        return $this->auditorium;
    }

    public function setAuditorium(?string $auditorium): static
    {
        $this->auditorium = $auditorium;

        return $this;
    }

    public function getCapacity(): ?string
    {
        return $this->capacity;
    }

    public function setCapacity(?string $capacity): static
    {
        $this->capacity = $capacity;

        return $this;
    }
    public function getLocalDate(): ?\DateTimeImmutable
    {
        return $this->localDate;
    }

    public function setLocalDate(\DateTimeImmutable $localDate): static
    {
        $this->localDate = $localDate;

        return $this;
    }

}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the entity builders and the test helper**

Every test builds its data with these builders (Arrange step), then stores it with `StoresEntities::store()`. No repository test is written: repositories are covered through the behavior tests of the planner and of the sync.

`tests/StoresEntities.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests;

use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;

/**
 * Persists entities built by the test builders. The kernel must already be booted.
 */
trait StoresEntities
{
    protected function store(object ...$entities): void
    {
        $em = static::getContainer()->get(EntityManagerInterface::class);
        foreach ($entities as $entity) {
            $em->persist($entity);
        }
        $em->flush();
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Builder;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Cinema;
use App\Catalog\Entity\City;

final class CinemaBuilder
{
    private string $slug = 'cinema-pathe-dijon';
    private ?string $name = null;
    private ?City $city = null;
    private float $latitude = 47.318031;
    private float $longitude = 5.029935;
    private string $timezone = 'Europe/Paris';
    private bool $open = true;

    public static function aCinema(): self
    {
        return new self();
    }

    public function withSlug(string $slug): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->slug = $slug;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function named(string $name): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->name = $name;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function in(City $city): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->city = $city;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function at(float $latitude, float $longitude): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->latitude = $latitude;
        $clone->longitude = $longitude;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function inTimezone(string $timezone): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->timezone = $timezone;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function closed(): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->open = false;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function build(): Cinema
    {
        if (null === $this->city) {
            throw new \LogicException('A cinema needs a city: call in() first.');
        }

        return (new Cinema())
            ->setSlug($this->slug)
            ->setName($this->name ?? 'Cinema '.$this->slug)
            ->setChain('pathe')
            ->setCountry('FR')
            ->setTimezone($this->timezone)
            ->setCity($this->city)
            ->setLatitude($this->latitude)
            ->setLongitude($this->longitude)
            ->setOpen($this->open);
    }
```

`tests/Builder/ShowtimeBuilder.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Builder;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Cinema;
use App\Catalog\Entity\Film;
use App\Catalog\Entity\Showtime;

final class ShowtimeBuilder
{
    private ?string $id = null;
    private ?Film $film = null;
    private ?Cinema $cinema = null;
    private string $start = '2030-01-10 14:00:00';
    private string $version = 'vf';
    private ?string $bookableUntil = null;

    public static function aShowtime(): self
    {
        return new self();
    }

    public function withId(string $id): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->id = $id;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function of(Film $film): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->film = $film;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function at(Cinema $cinema): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->cinema = $cinema;

        return $clone;
    }

    /**
     * @param string $start session start (ads included) in the cinema's local time, e.g. '2030-01-10 14:00:00'
     */
    public function startingAt(string $start): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->start = $start;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function inVersion(string $version): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->version = $version;

        return $clone;
    }

    /**
     * @param string $moment UTC instant, e.g. '2020-01-01 00:00:00'
     */
    public function bookableUntil(string $moment): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->bookableUntil = $moment;

        return $clone;
    }

    public function build(): Showtime
    {
        if (null === $this->film || null === $this->cinema) {
            throw new \LogicException('A showtime needs a film and a cinema: call of() and at() first.');
        }

        $local = new \DateTimeImmutable($this->start, new \DateTimeZone($this->cinema->getTimezone()));
        $start = $local->setTimezone(new \DateTimeZone('UTC'));
        $id = $this->id ?? 'V1S'.abs(crc32($this->film->getSlug().$this->cinema->getSlug().$this->start));

        return (new Showtime())
            ->setId($id)
            ->setFilm($this->film)
            ->setCinema($this->cinema)
            ->setStartsAt($start)
            ->setEndsAt($start->modify('+'.($this->film->getDuration() + 20).' minutes'))
            ->setLocalDate(new \DateTimeImmutable($local->format('Y-m-d')))
            ->setVersion($this->version)
            ->setStatus('available')
            ->setBookingUrl('https://s.pathe.fr/fr/'.$id.'/booking')
            ->setReservableUntil(new \DateTimeImmutable($this->bookableUntil ?? $start->modify('+20 minutes')->format('Y-m-d H:i:s')));
    }
}
```

Dates default to 2030 so that showtimes stay bookable whatever the day the tests run. `startingAt()` takes the cinema's local time (as Pathé gives it); the builder stores UTC, like the sync does.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the repositories**

`src/Catalog/Repository/CityRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Repository;

use App\Catalog\Entity\City;
use Doctrine\Bundle\DoctrineBundle\Repository\ServiceEntityRepository;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;

/**
 * @extends ServiceEntityRepository<City>
 */
class CityRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, City::class);
    }

    /**
     * @return array list of ['slug' => ..., 'name' => ...] sorted by name
     */
    public function findAllForSelect(): array
    {
        return $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
            ->select('c.slug', 'c.name')
            ->orderBy('c.name', 'ASC')
            ->getQuery()
            ->getArrayResult();
    }

    /**
     * @return array|null ['slug' => ..., 'name' => ...] or null if the city is unknown
     */
    public function findBySlug(string $slug): ?array
    {
        $rows = $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
            ->select('c.slug', 'c.name')
            ->where('c.slug = :slug')
            ->setParameter('slug', $slug)
            ->getQuery()
            ->getArrayResult();

        return $rows[0] ?? null;
    }
}
```

`src/Catalog/Repository/CinemaRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Repository;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Cinema;
use Doctrine\Bundle\DoctrineBundle\Repository\ServiceEntityRepository;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;

/**
 * @extends ServiceEntityRepository<Cinema>
 */
class CinemaRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, Cinema::class);
    }

    /**
     * Open cinemas whose position is known.
     *
     * @return array list of ['slug', 'name', 'citySlug', 'timezone', 'latitude', 'longitude']
     */
    public function findOpenWithCoordinates(): array
    {
        return $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
            ->select('c.slug', 'c.name', 'IDENTITY(c.city) AS citySlug', 'c.timezone', 'c.latitude', 'c.longitude')
            ->where('c.open = true')
            ->andWhere('c.latitude IS NOT NULL')
            ->andWhere('c.longitude IS NOT NULL')
            ->orderBy('c.slug', 'ASC')
            ->getQuery()
            ->getArrayResult();
    }

    /**
     * @return array list of ['slug', 'name', 'citySlug', 'timezone', 'latitude', 'longitude']
     */
    public function findByCity(string $citySlug): array
    {
        return $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
            ->select('c.slug', 'c.name', 'IDENTITY(c.city) AS citySlug', 'c.timezone', 'c.latitude', 'c.longitude')
            ->where('IDENTITY(c.city) = :city')
            ->andWhere('c.open = true')
            ->setParameter('city', $citySlug)
            ->orderBy('c.slug', 'ASC')
            ->getQuery()
            ->getArrayResult();
    }
}
```

`src/Catalog/Repository/FilmRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Repository;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Film;
use Doctrine\Bundle\DoctrineBundle\Repository\ServiceEntityRepository;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;

/**
 * @extends ServiceEntityRepository<Film>
 */
class FilmRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, Film::class);
    }

    /**
     * @return array|null ['slug', 'title', 'duration', 'releaseDate', 'genres', 'posterUrl', 'contentRating'] or null
     */
    public function findBySlug(string $slug): ?array
    {
        $rows = $this->findBySlugs([$slug]);

        return $rows[0] ?? null;
    }

    /**
     * @param array  $slugs     list of slugs
     * @param string $direction 'ASC' or 'DESC' (sorted by title)
     *
     * @return array list of films (same shape as findBySlug)
     */
    public function findBySlugs(array $slugs, string $direction = 'ASC'): array
    {
        if ([] === $slugs) {
            return [];
        }
        if ('ASC' !== $direction && 'DESC' !== $direction) {
            throw new \InvalidArgumentException('Invalid sort direction: '.$direction);
        }

        return $this->createQueryBuilder('f')
            ->select('f.slug', 'f.title', 'f.duration', 'f.releaseDate', 'f.genres', 'f.posterUrl', 'f.contentRating')
            ->where('f.slug IN (:slugs)')
            ->setParameter('slugs', $slugs)
            ->orderBy('f.title', $direction)
            ->getQuery()
            ->getArrayResult();
    }
}
```

`src/Catalog/Repository/ShowtimeRepository.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Catalog\Repository;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Showtime;
use Doctrine\Bundle\DoctrineBundle\Repository\ServiceEntityRepository;
use Doctrine\DBAL\ArrayParameterType;
use Doctrine\Persistence\ManagerRegistry;

/**
 * @extends ServiceEntityRepository<Showtime>
 */
class ShowtimeRepository extends ServiceEntityRepository
{
    public function __construct(ManagerRegistry $registry)
    {
        parent::__construct($registry, Showtime::class);
    }

    /**
     * Local days (format 'Y-m-d', in each cinema's time zone) that have at least one showtime, from $from inclusive.
     */
    public function findAvailableDates(string $from): array
    {
        return $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->fetchFirstColumn(
            'SELECT DISTINCT local_date FROM showtime WHERE local_date >= :from ORDER BY local_date',
            ['from' => $from],
        );
    }

    /**
     * Showtimes of one day in the given cinemas, bookable at instant $now, excluding the excluded films.
     *
     * @param string      $date              local day of the cinemas, format 'Y-m-d'
     * @param array       $cinemaSlugs       slugs of the cinemas to keep
     * @param array       $excludedFilmSlugs slugs of the films to exclude (already seen)
     * @param string|null $version           'vf', 'vost', 'vo', 'vfst' or null for any
     * @param string      $now               current instant in UTC, format 'Y-m-d H:i:s'
     *
     * @return array rows ['id', 'filmSlug', 'filmTitle', 'duration', 'cinemaSlug', 'cinemaName', 'timezone',
     *               'latitude', 'longitude', 'startsAt', 'endsAt', 'version', 'bookingUrl'] (instants in UTC)
     */
    public function findCandidates(string $date, array $cinemaSlugs, array $excludedFilmSlugs, ?string $version, string $now): array
    {
        if ([] === $cinemaSlugs) {
            return [];
        }

        $sql = 'SELECT s.id, s.film_slug AS filmSlug, f.title AS filmTitle, f.duration, s.cinema_slug AS cinemaSlug,
                       c.name AS cinemaName, c.timezone, c.latitude, c.longitude, s.starts_at AS startsAt, s.ends_at AS endsAt,
                       s.version, s.booking_url AS bookingUrl
                FROM showtime s
                INNER JOIN film f ON f.slug = s.film_slug
                INNER JOIN cinema c ON c.slug = s.cinema_slug
                WHERE s.local_date = :date
                  AND s.cinema_slug IN (:cinemas)
                  AND s.status = :status
                  AND (s.reservable_until IS NULL OR s.reservable_until > :now)';
        $params = [
            'date' => $date,
            'cinemas' => $cinemaSlugs,
            'status' => 'available',
            'now' => $now,
        ];
        $types = ['cinemas' => ArrayParameterType::STRING];

        if ([] !== $excludedFilmSlugs) {
            $sql .= ' AND s.film_slug NOT IN (:excluded)';
            $params['excluded'] = $excludedFilmSlugs;
            $types['excluded'] = ArrayParameterType::STRING;
        }
        if (null !== $version) {
            $sql .= ' AND s.version = :version';
            $params['version'] = $version;
        }

        return $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->fetchAllAssociative($sql.' ORDER BY s.starts_at, s.id', $params, $types);
    }

    /**
     * Deletes the showtimes of the given cinemas whose local day is between $from and $to (inclusive),
     * except those whose identifier is kept.
     *
     * @return int number of showtimes deleted
     */
    public function deleteForCinemasBetween(array $cinemaSlugs, string $from, string $to, array $keepIds): int
    {
        if ([] === $cinemaSlugs) {
            return 0;
        }

        $sql = 'DELETE FROM showtime WHERE cinema_slug IN (:cinemas) AND local_date BETWEEN :from AND :to';
        $params = ['cinemas' => $cinemaSlugs, 'from' => $from, 'to' => $to];
        $types = ['cinemas' => ArrayParameterType::STRING];
        if ([] !== $keepIds) {
            $sql .= ' AND id NOT IN (:keep)';
            $params['keep'] = $keepIds;
            $types['keep'] = ArrayParameterType::STRING;
        }

        return (int) $this->getEntityManager()->getConnection()->executeStatement($sql, $params, $types);
    }
}
```

### Task 3: Pathé SDK and catalog sync (`catalog:sync`)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/Sdk/Pathe/PatheClient.php`, `src/Sdk/Pathe/PatheMapper.php`, `src/Sdk/Pathe/README.md` (third-party SDK written like a standalone library: everything that talks to Pathé, on PSR interfaces only)
- Create: `src/Catalog/Sync/CatalogSynchronizer.php`, `src/Catalog/Command/SyncCommand.php`
- Create: `config/packages/chains.yaml`
- Modify: `config/packages/framework.yaml` (scoped HTTP client, `cache.pathe` pool), `config/services.yaml` (SDK wiring; fake Pathé API in the test environment), `composer.json`, `.env`, `.env.test`
- Create: `tests/Builder/PatheApiBuilder.php`, `tests/Fake/FakePatheApi.php`
- Test: `tests/Integration/Catalog/Sync/CatalogSyncTest.php`, `tests/Integration/Catalog/Command/SyncCommandTest.php`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (entities, `ShowtimeRepository::deleteForCinemasBetween()`).
- Produces:
  - Parameter `app.chains` (array keyed by chain id): `pathe: { name: 'Pathé', country: 'FR', timezone: 'Europe/Paris' }`. Every chain-specific conversion reads it; adding a chain later starts here.
  - `PatheClient::__construct(?ClientInterface $httpClient = null, ?RequestFactoryInterface $requestFactory = null, ?CacheItemPoolInterface $cache = null, ?LoggerInterface $logger = null, int $delayMs = 1000, int $cacheTtl = 3600, string $baseUrl = PatheClient::BASE_URL)` (PSR-18, PSR-17, PSR-6, PSR-3; a missing collaborator is discovered with psr-discovery); `getCities(): array|false`, `getCinemas(): array|false`, `getShows(): array|false` (the `shows` list), `getCinemaProgramme(string $cinemaSlug): array|false`, `getShowtimes(string $showSlug, string $cinemaSlug): array|false`. `false` = one-off failure (logged); `\RuntimeException` on 403/429 (Pathé is blocking us: the sync must stop).
  - `PatheMapper::mapCity(array $raw): array` → `['slug','name']`; `mapCinema(array $raw): array` → `['slug','name','citySlug','open','address','postalCode','town','latitude','longitude','hallCount']`; `mapFilm(array $raw): array|false` (`false` for events); `mapShowtimes(array $raw, string $timezone): array` → list of `['id','startsAt','endsAt','localDate','version','status','bookingUrl','reservableUntil','auditorium','capacity']` (instants converted to UTC); `showSlugsPlayingBetween(array $programme, string $from, string $to): array`.
  - `CatalogSynchronizer::synchronize(array $citySlugs, ?string $today = null, int $days = 7): array|false` → `['cities','cinemas','films','showtimes','deleted','errors']`, or `false` if `/cities`, `/cinemas` or `/shows` is unreachable. `$today` is a local day of the chain; `null` = today in the chain's time zone. Lets the client's `\RuntimeException` through.
  - Command `catalog:sync [--city=paris --city=lyon]`; default cities: `%env(PATHE_CITIES)%` (`paris,lyon,dijon`).
  - Test doubles: `PatheApiBuilder::aPatheApi()->withCity()->withCinema()->withFilm()->withEvent()->withShowtime()->failing()` builds the responses of a fake Pathé API with the real shapes; `FakePatheApi` (a PSR-18 client given to the SDK in the test environment) serves them with `serve(PatheApiBuilder)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Declare the chain, install the PSR packages and wire the SDK**

`config/packages/chains.yaml`:

```yaml
parameters:
    # Supported cinema chains. Instants are stored in UTC; each chain's time zone converts
    # its local times (input from its API, display) and gives the country of its cinemas.
    app.chains:
        pathe:
            name: 'Pathé'
            country: 'FR'
            timezone: 'Europe/Paris'
```

In the SDK, everything goes through PSR interfaces. Install the interfaces, the discovery packages and a PSR-7/PSR-17 implementation for the application:

Run: `make composer c="require psr/http-client psr/http-factory psr/cache psr/log psr-discovery/http-client-implementations psr-discovery/http-factory-implementations psr-discovery/cache-implementations psr-discovery/log-implementations nyholm/psr7"`
Expected: the `nyholm/psr7` recipe creates `config/packages/nyholm_psr7.yaml`. It aliases `Psr\Http\Message\RequestFactoryInterface` and the other PSR-17 factories to `Nyholm\Psr7\Factory\Psr17Factory`.

In `config/packages/framework.yaml`, under `framework:`:

```yaml
    http_client:
        scoped_clients:
            # Transport settings only: the SDK builds its URLs and headers itself.
            pathe.http_client:
                scope: '^https://www\.pathe\.fr/'
                timeout: 10
    cache:
        pools:
            # Pathé reference data (cities, cinemas, films), kept PATHE_CACHE_TTL seconds.
            cache.pathe:
                adapter: cache.app
```

In `config/services.yaml`, under `services:`:

```yaml
    # PSR-18 adapter over the scoped client: the SDK only knows PSR interfaces.
    app.pathe_http_client:
        class: Symfony\Component\HttpClient\Psr18Client
        arguments: ['@pathe.http_client']

    # The PSR-17 request factory (nyholm/psr7) and the PSR-3 logger (monolog) are autowired.
    App\Sdk\Pathe\PatheClient:
        arguments:
            $httpClient: '@app.pathe_http_client'
            $cache: '@cache.pathe'
            $delayMs: '%env(int:PATHE_DELAY_MS)%'
            $cacheTtl: '%env(int:PATHE_CACHE_TTL)%'
```

and at the end of the file:

```yaml
when@test:
    services:
        App\Tests\Fake\FakePatheApi:
            public: true
        # The SDK talks to the fake Pathé API (a PSR-18 client) instead of pathe.fr.
        app.pathe_http_client:
            alias: App\Tests\Fake\FakePatheApi
```

(If `when@test` already exists in this file, merge the keys.) In `.env`: `PATHE_DELAY_MS=1000`, `PATHE_CACHE_TTL=3600` and `PATHE_CITIES=paris,lyon,dijon`. In `.env.test`: `PATHE_DELAY_MS=0`, `PATHE_CACHE_TTL=0` (no cache: each sync of a test reads the fake API) and `PATHE_CITIES=dijon`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the fake Pathé API and its builder**

`tests/Fake/FakePatheApi.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Fake;

use App\Tests\Builder\PatheApiBuilder;
use Nyholm\Psr7\Response;
use Psr\Http\Client\ClientInterface;
use Psr\Http\Message\RequestInterface;
use Psr\Http\Message\ResponseInterface;

/**
 * PSR-18 client that replaces pathe.fr in the test environment (it is given to the SDK in place of the real one).
 * Any path that was not served answers 404, like Pathé does for an unknown slug.
 */
final class FakePatheApi implements ClientInterface
{
    /** @var array<string, array{0: string, 1: int}> */
    private array $responses = [];

    public function serve(PatheApiBuilder $api): void
    {
        $this->responses = $api->build();
    }

    public function sendRequest(RequestInterface $request): ResponseInterface
    {
        $path = substr($request->getUri()->getPath(), \strlen('/api/'));
        [$body, $status] = $this->responses[$path] ?? ['"Object with slug = '.$path.' doesn\'t exist"', 404];

        return new Response($status, ['Content-Type' => 'application/json'], $body);
    }
}
```

`tests/Builder/PatheApiBuilder.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Builder;

/**
 * Builds the responses of a fake Pathé API with the shapes of the real one (checked on
 * October 4, 2026): latitude in "x", longitude in "y", contentRating [] when unrated,
 * naive local times, film end = start + running time + 20 minutes of ads.
 */
final class PatheApiBuilder
{
    private array $cities = [];
    private array $cinemas = [];
    private array $shows = [];
    private array $programmes = [];
    private array $showtimes = [];
    /** @var array<string, array{0: string, 1: int}> */
    private array $overrides = [];

    public static function aPatheApi(): self
    {
        return new self();
    }

    public function withCity(string $slug, string $name): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->cities[] = [
            'id' => \count($this->cities) + 1,
            'slug' => $slug,
            'name' => $name,
            'departement' => '21',
            'isIdf' => false,
            // Pathé's city positions are unreliable: the application never uses them.
            'gpsPosition' => ['x' => 0.0, 'y' => 0.0],
            'cinemas' => [],
        ];

        return $clone;
    }

    public function withCinema(string $slug, string $citySlug, float $latitude, float $longitude, bool $open = true): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->cinemas[] = [
            'slug' => $slug,
            'citySlug' => $citySlug,
            'name' => 'Cinema '.$slug,
            'status' => $open,
            'theaters' => [[
                'name' => 'Cinema '.$slug,
                'addressLine1' => '1 Main Street',
                'addressLine2' => '',
                'addressZip' => '21000',
                'addressCity' => 'Dijon',
                // Pathé puts the latitude in "x" and the longitude in "y".
                'gpsPosition' => ['x' => $latitude, 'y' => $longitude],
            ]],
            'hallCount' => 9,
        ];
        $clone->programmes[$slug] = ['days' => [], 'shows' => []];

        return $clone;
    }

    public function withFilm(string $slug, string $title, int $duration): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->shows[$slug] = [
            'slug' => $slug,
            'title' => $title,
            'duration' => $duration,
            'releaseAt' => ['FR_FR' => '2026-09-30'],
            'genres' => ['Action'],
            'posterPath' => null,
            // Pathé sends an empty array, not null, while a film has no rating yet.
            'contentRating' => [],
            'isMovie' => true,
        ];

        return $clone;
    }

    public function withEvent(string $slug, string $title): self
    {
        $clone = $this->withFilm($slug, $title, 40);
        $clone->shows[$slug]['isMovie'] = false;

        return $clone;
    }

    /**
     * @param string      $localTime  session start as Pathé sends it: naive local time, e.g. '2026-10-04 21:40:00'
     * @param string      $sessionRef Pathé session reference, e.g. 'V3345S85501'
     * @param string|null $bookingUrl overrides the booking link built from $sessionRef
     */
    public function withShowtime(string $filmSlug, string $cinemaSlug, string $localTime, string $sessionRef, ?string $bookingUrl = null): self
    {
        if (!isset($this->shows[$filmSlug], $this->programmes[$cinemaSlug])) {
            throw new \LogicException('Declare the film and the cinema before their showtimes.');
        }

        $clone = clone $this;
        $start = new \DateTimeImmutable($localTime, new \DateTimeZone('Europe/Paris'));
        $day = $start->format('Y-m-d');
        $clone->programmes[$cinemaSlug]['days'][$day] = ['upsell' => null, 'highlightedShowtime' => null];
        $clone->programmes[$cinemaSlug]['shows'][$filmSlug]['days'][$day] = ['tags' => ['DEFAULT'], 'bookable' => true, 'versions' => ['vf']];
        $clone->showtimes[$filmSlug.'|'.$cinemaSlug][$day][] = [
            'time' => $start->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
            'version' => 'vf',
            'tags' => ['DEFAULT'],
            'status' => 'available',
            'reservabilityEnd' => $start->modify('+20 minutes')->format(\DATE_ATOM),
            'isMovie' => true,
            'refCmd' => $bookingUrl ?? 'https://s.pathe.fr/fr/'.$sessionRef.'/booking',
            'auditoriumName' => '1',
            'auditoriumCapacity' => '244',
            'endTime' => $start->modify('+'.($this->shows[$filmSlug]['duration'] + 20).' minutes')->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'),
        ];

        return $clone;
    }

    public function failing(string $path, int $status, string $body = '"error"'): self
    {
        $clone = clone $this;
        $clone->overrides[$path] = [$body, $status];

        return $clone;
    }

    /**
     * @return array<string, array{0: string, 1: int}> response body and HTTP status, keyed by API path
     */
    public function build(): array
    {
        $responses = [
            'cities' => [json_encode($this->cities, \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 200],
            'cinemas' => [json_encode($this->cinemas, \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 200],
            'shows' => [json_encode(['shows' => array_values($this->shows), 'labels' => [], 'contentratings' => []], \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 200],
        ];
        foreach ($this->programmes as $cinemaSlug => $programme) {
            $responses['cinema/'.$cinemaSlug.'/shows'] = [json_encode($programme, \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 200];
        }
        foreach ($this->showtimes as $pair => $byDay) {
            [$filmSlug, $cinemaSlug] = explode('|', $pair);
            $responses['show/'.$filmSlug.'/showtimes/'.$cinemaSlug] = [json_encode($byDay, \JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR), 200];
        }

        return array_replace($responses, $this->overrides);
    }
}
```

`tests/Integration/Catalog/Sync/CatalogSyncTest.php`:

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Integration\Catalog\Sync;

use App\Catalog\Entity\Cinema;
use App\Catalog\Entity\City;
use App\Catalog\Entity\Film;
use App\Catalog\Entity\Showtime;
use App\Catalog\Sync\CatalogSynchronizer;
use App\Tests\Builder\PatheApiBuilder;
use App\Tests\Fake\FakePatheApi;
use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
use Symfony\Bundle\FrameworkBundle\Test\KernelTestCase;

final class CatalogSyncTest extends KernelTestCase
{
    private function dijon(): PatheApiBuilder
    {
        return PatheApiBuilder::aPatheApi()
            ->withCity('dijon', 'Dijon')
            ->withCity('lyon', 'Lyon')
            ->withCinema('cinema-pathe-dijon', 'dijon', 47.318031, 5.029935)
            ->withCinema('cinema-pathe-vaise', 'lyon', 45.7746, 4.8048)
            ->withFilm('digger-51293', 'Digger', 129)
            ->withFilm('verity-50815', 'Verity', 117)
            ->withEvent('ma-mini-seance-55477', 'Ma Mini-Séance')
            ->withShowtime('digger-51293', 'cinema-pathe-dijon', '2026-10-04 21:40:00', 'V3345S85501')
            ->withShowtime('verity-50815', 'cinema-pathe-dijon', '2026-10-04 16:30:00', 'V3345S85483');
    }

    private function synchronize(PatheApiBuilder $api): array|false
    {
        self::getContainer()->get(FakePatheApi::class)->serve($api);

        return self::getContainer()->get(CatalogSynchronizer::class)->synchronize(['dijon'], '2026-10-04');
    }

    private function em(): EntityManagerInterface
    {
        return self::getContainer()->get(EntityManagerInterface::class);
    }

    public function testCopiesTheRequestedCitiesIntoTheCatalogWithInstantsInUtc(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        self::bootKernel();
        $api = $this->dijon()->withShowtime('digger-51293', 'cinema-pathe-dijon', '2026-10-04 14:00:00', 'V3345S85470', 'https://evil.example/fr/V3345S85470/booking');

        // Act
        $stats = $this->synchronize($api);

        // Assert
        self::assertSame(0, $stats['errors']);
        self::assertSame(['dijon'], array_map(static fn (City $city) => $city->getSlug(), $this->em()->getRepository(City::class)->findAll()));
        $cinema = $this->em()->find(Cinema::class, 'cinema-pathe-dijon');
        self::assertEqualsWithDelta(47.318031, $cinema->getLatitude(), 0.000001, 'Pathé "x" is the latitude');
        self::assertSame('Europe/Paris', $cinema->getTimezone());
        self::assertSame('FR', $cinema->getCountry());
        self::assertNull($this->em()->find(Film::class, 'ma-mini-seance-55477'), 'events are not films');
        $late = $this->em()->find(Showtime::class, 'V3345S85501');
        self::assertSame('2026-10-04 19:40:00', $late->getStartsAt()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'), '21:40 in Paris (summer time) is 19:40 UTC');
        self::assertSame('2026-10-04 22:09:00', $late->getEndsAt()->format('Y-m-d H:i:s'), 'the film ends at 00:09, Paris time');
        self::assertSame('2026-10-04', $late->getLocalDate()->format('Y-m-d'), 'it still belongs to October 4 locally');
        self::assertNull($this->em()->find(Showtime::class, 'V3345S85470'), 'a booking link outside s.pathe.fr is never stored');
    }

    public function testASecondSyncRemovesTheShowtimesThatDisappeared(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        self::bootKernel();
        $this->synchronize($this->dijon());
        $withoutVerity = PatheApiBuilder::aPatheApi()
            ->withCity('dijon', 'Dijon')
            ->withCinema('cinema-pathe-dijon', 'dijon', 47.318031, 5.029935)
            ->withFilm('digger-51293', 'Digger', 129)
            ->withFilm('verity-50815', 'Verity', 117)
            ->withShowtime('digger-51293', 'cinema-pathe-dijon', '2026-10-04 21:40:00', 'V3345S85501');

        // Act
        $stats = $this->synchronize($withoutVerity);

        // Assert
        $this->em()->clear();
        self::assertSame(1, $stats['deleted']);
        self::assertSame(1, $this->em()->getRepository(Showtime::class)->count([]));
        self::assertNotNull($this->em()->find(Showtime::class, 'V3345S85501'));
    }

    public function testKeepsTheShowtimesOfACinemaWhoseScheduleCouldNotBeFullyRead(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        self::bootKernel();
        $this->synchronize($this->dijon());
        $partlyFailing = $this->dijon()->failing('show/verity-50815/showtimes/cinema-pathe-dijon', 500);

        // Act
        $stats = $this->synchronize($partlyFailing);

        // Assert
        $this->em()->clear();
        self::assertSame(1, $stats['errors']);
        self::assertSame(0, $stats['deleted']);
        self::assertNotNull($this->em()->find(Showtime::class, 'V3345S85483'));
    }
}
```

```php
<?php

namespace App\Tests\Integration\Catalog\Command;

use App\Tests\Builder\PatheApiBuilder;
use App\Tests\Fake\FakePatheApi;
use Symfony\Bundle\FrameworkBundle\Console\Application;
use Symfony\Bundle\FrameworkBundle\Test\KernelTestCase;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Command\Command;
use Symfony\Component\Console\Tester\CommandTester;

final class SyncCommandTest extends KernelTestCase
{
    public function testReportsWhatWasSynchronized(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        $kernel = self::bootKernel();
        self::getContainer()->get(FakePatheApi::class)->serve(PatheApiBuilder::aPatheApi()
            ->withCity('dijon', 'Dijon')
            ->withCinema('cinema-pathe-dijon', 'dijon', 47.318031, 5.029935)
            ->withFilm('digger-51293', 'Digger', 129));
        $tester = new CommandTester((new Application($kernel))->find('catalog:sync'));

        // Act
        $tester->execute(['--city' => ['dijon']]);

        // Assert
        self::assertSame(Command::SUCCESS, $tester->getStatusCode());
        self::assertStringContainsString('1 cities, 1 cinemas, 1 films', $tester->getDisplay());
    }

    public function testStopsCleanlyWhenPatheBlocksUs(): void
    {
        // Arrange
        $kernel = self::bootKernel();
        self::getContainer()->get(FakePatheApi::class)->serve(PatheApiBuilder::aPatheApi()->failing('cities', 403, '{"error":"Error from IP 1.2.3.4"}'));
        $tester = new CommandTester((new Application($kernel))->find('catalog:sync'));

        // Act
        $tester->execute(['--city' => ['dijon']]);

        // Assert
        self::assertSame(Command::FAILURE, $tester->getStatusCode());
        self::assertStringContainsString('Pathé refused the request', $tester->getDisplay());
    }
}
```
